#5199. Cardano Hits 57.6% Staking Bid While Price Compresses

Home » Cardano (ADA) » Cardano Hits 57.6% Staking Bid While Price Compresses

ADA price continues to bounce around the historical demand points while staking sees a new high.

Cardano continues to show strong on-chain participation even as ADA trades near multi-month lows. The Cardano Foundation noted that 57.6% of circulating supply is currently staked, with roughly 21.62 billion ADA delegated to stake pools.

The Foundation highlighted that every staked ADA backs a pool involved in block production and L-1 network security. Cardano’s liquid staking model lets holders retain full control of their tokens, including the ability to spend or move them at any time.

57.6% of circulating $ADA is staked.Every staked coin backs a stake pool that helps produce blocks and secure the network. Cardano’s liquid staking allows holders to keep full control and spend or move their funds anytime.That’s how a network stays resilient. https://t.co/b1zWSViLmy pic.twitter.com/ip0D0a66kC

The design has kept participation rates high relative to many other proof-of-stake networks, supporting resilience even during weaker price periods.

On the technical side, pattern analyst Crypto With Gopal observed a falling wedge pattern forming on the 15-minute chart. ADA has been consolidating between roughly $0.172 and $0.173 as selling pressure eases.

$ADA / USDT 👀Cardano is forming a Falling Wedge pattern on the 15-min chart! Price is consolidating near $0.172–$0.173 as selling pressure gradually weakens.A breakout above $0.176 could trigger a bullish move towards $0.180–$0.195 📈🔥Compression is building, and bulls… pic.twitter.com/4VQvgbGuVB

A move above $0.176 could open a path toward $0.180–$0.195 in the near term, according to the analysis. The setup reflects tightening price action after a period of decline, though confirmation still depends on a sustained break higher.

Gopal’s outlook focuses on the immediate recovery zone above current prices. Beyond that range, some longer-term scenarios place $0.50 as a more extended target if broader market conditions improve and network activity continues to expand.

Hitting that level would require ADA to more than double from present levels and clear intermediate resistance that has limited upside in recent months.

The contrast remains clear. ADA staking participation near 58% of circulating supply indicates continued holder commitment to securing the network, while price action has compressed near the $0.17 area.

For now, the network’s security metrics and the short-term technical structure are the primary points of focus.

#5198. Solana (SOL) to Connect With Wall Street’s $4.7 Quadrillion Clearing Giant

Disclaimer: The opinions expressed by our writers are their own and do not represent the views of U.Today. The financial and market information provided on U.Today is intended for informational purposes only. U.Today is not liable for any financial losses incurred while trading cryptocurrencies. Conduct your own research by contacting financial experts before making any investment decisions. We believe that all content is accurate as of the date of publication, but certain offers mentioned may no longer be available.

The Depository Trust & Clearing Corporation (DTCC) will connect its tokenization service to Solana (SOL) in the second half of 2027, the company said on Nov. 12.

The integration will allow "digital twins" of Russell 1000 stocks, major ETFs and U.S. Treasuries to be issued on the Solana blockchain. In December 2025, the SEC issued a no-action letter that allowed DTC to tokenize these assets.

In 2025, DTCC processed $4.7 quadrillion in securities transactions. Its depository, DTC, holds and services $114 trillion in assets from more than 150 countries.

The DTCC Tokenization Service (DTS) will let large market participants convert conventional securities into tokens, move them across the Solana network and redeem them.

"DTC-tokenized assets made available on Solana – and all blockchain networks integrated with the service – will retain the same investor protections, ownership rights and regulatory safeguards that apply to traditionally held securities," said Brian Steele, president of DTCC Clearing & Securities Services.

According to DTCC CEO Frank La Salla, the integration will also free up securities for use as collateral.

DTS went live on Oct. 28, 2026. At launch, it worked with Canton Network and a private ledger. DTCC said that it adds new networks at clients' request.

"DTCC is committed to providing an open and connected platform that provides our users with access to mature distributed ledger technology networks like Solana that have established performance, security and governance features," said Nadine Chakar, global head of DTCC Digital Assets.

Nick Ducoff, general manager for institutional at the Solana Foundation, also commented on the news. "Institutions choose Solana because it already runs real financial activity at scale," he said.

According to Ducoff, the integration "connects one of the most trusted names in post-trade infrastructure with a network built for always-on markets."

As of Nov. 12, distributed real-world assets (RWA) in the Solana ecosystem reached $5.13 billion across more than 1.02 million holders, according to RWA.xyz. Transfers over the past 30 days totaled $16.48 billion.

The stablecoin market cap on Solana stands at $13.85 billion, with monthly turnover of $387.41 billion.

#5197. Near AI Breach Exposes User Details and Data From 18 AI Agents

Near AI Breach Exposes User Details and Data From 18 AI Agents

Near AI has disclosed a breach that exposed account information belonging to about 100 users and data from 18 AI agents after an attacker exploited software running its servers.

The company said in a November 9 report that an attacker used a remote code execution vulnerability in SGLang, an open-source engine that runs AI models and generates responses to user requests.

The exploit allowed the intruder to execute commands on one inference server and then reach other parts of Near AI’s infrastructure.

Investigators traced the intrusion to an internal system connected to Private Chat, a feature the company had already retired. The attacker downloaded names and email addresses for approximately 100 users, along with subscription plan and account balance information for some of them.

Another 18 agents were accessed on an older deployment of Near AI Agents, where the attacker retrieved their chat histories, settings and logs. Those agents were not running inside NEAR AI’s confidential computing environment, which is designed to shield information from the company operating the servers.

Logs also exposed NEAR AI Cloud API keys belonging to two customers. These keys function like digital credentials that applications use to access cloud services; Near AI says it has revoked both, but did not say whether the attacker used them.

Near AI found no indication that stored Private Chat conversations or uploaded files had been accessed. The confirmed theft involved account records and legacy agent data, while exposure of live confidential requests remains a possibility identified by the investigation.

Hardware isolation does not guarantee that the software inside the protected environment is safe. “It does not protect against a vulnerability in the software running inside the environment,” Near AI acknowledged, explaining that the SGLang flaw let the attacker run code where confidential requests were being processed.

SGLang had already attracted scrutiny from security researchers. In July, Carnegie Mellon University’s CERT Coordination Center documented six vulnerabilities in the project, including bugs that could let attackers execute commands or expose credentials, depending on how servers were configured.

Near AI did not identify the specific SGLang flaw exploited in this incident, so there is no basis to link the breach to any of those earlier disclosures. It reported the vulnerability to SGLang’s developers.

Near, to its credit, was quick to fix the issues.

“On detection, we revoked all active sessions and administrative credentials, replaced all agent tokens, took Private Chat offline, and deployed a fix for the SGLang vulnerability across all inference servers,” the project said.

”We've completed the rotation of all remaining credentials and a full forensic review. We have notified users whose account details or agents we have confirmed were accessed and will make any regulatory notifications that are required.”

Near AI notified users whose account records or agents it confirmed were accessed and says it will make any required regulatory notifications. It also warned users to be wary of unsolicited messages referring to their Near AI accounts or balances, and contacted the owners of all 18 affected agents individually.

#5196. Ethereum’s store-of-value narrative is gaining traction – From Joseph Lubin, at least

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Ethereum’s “store-of-value” narrative is gaining traction lately.

Looking at the broader picture, there are several factors that are leading to increased risk sentiment in the U.S. market.

Whether it be fears over an AI bubble, high-interest rates, CPI figures, or the aftermath of the mid-term elections, Bitcoin is heading toward its worst annual cycle since the 2022 bear market, with year-to-date losses approaching 17% in 2026.

Keeping this in mind, while Ethereum has suffered nearly twice Bitcoin’s losses and is looking at its worst year since 2022, co-founder Joseph Lubin is bullish over the future potential of Ethereum as an inflationary hedge.

Highlighting several key trends on-chain, he says that Ethereum has long-term potential to outperform both Bitcoin and gold as a store of value.

One of the main reasons for his argument is because of the DeFi ecosystem, stablecoins, and smart contracts on Ethereum. These allow ETH to have a stronger utility base compared to Bitcoin and gold.

In addition, with AI adoption accelerating, Ethereum could also see rising demand for its network infrastructure.

Ethereum’s inflation-hedge narrative faces a key test.

As previously mentioned, the U.S. market is facing a range of risks. This has made this one of the most volatile times for investors. So far, Ethereum has had trouble attracting risk capital, falling almost 30% during Q4 compared to Bitcoin, which has fallen only 17%.

This puts Joseph Lubin’s thesis under pressure, as Bitcoin continues to lead the store-of-value narrative while Ethereum struggles to keep up.

Gold, meanwhile, is also losing steam. The metal is trading in a technical downtrend with losses of more than 5% so far in Q4, as it has failed to move above $4,000. The most recent fall below this level has caused a loss of around $480 billion in market value in just 10 hours.

That said, gold still has a clear “relative-strength” advantage over crypto.

As the chart above shows, the ETH/XAU ratio has lost more than 19% in Q4, demonstrating that Ethereum has failed to make any progress relative to gold. This becomes a key level to monitor. A breakout could result in better technical support for Lubin’s long-term thesis.

For now, Ethereum is struggling to outperform Bitcoin and gold currently. Despite rising staking participation and stronger network security, ETH is yet to translate these fundamentals into stronger capital inflows or relative price performance.

Ethereum is showing growing network participation, supporting Lubin’s store-of-value thesis.

Despite this, ETH continues to lag Bitcoin and gold, with the ETH/XAU ratio down over 19% in Q4.

#5195. Hyperliquid Burns $1.53 Million in HYPE Tokens in 24 Hours Amid Price Dip

Disclaimer: The opinions expressed by our writers are their own and do not represent the views of U.Today. The financial and market information provided on U.Today is intended for informational purposes only. U.Today is not liable for any financial losses incurred while trading cryptocurrencies. Conduct your own research by contacting financial experts before making any investment decisions. We believe that all content is accurate as of the date of publication, but certain offers mentioned may no longer be available.

Decentralized exchange Hyperliquid has bought back and burned 21,200 HYPE over the past 24 hours, spending $1.53 million on the purchase at an average price of $72.31, according to Onchain Lens.

The on-chain analytics platform also reported that Hyperliquid has permanently removed a total of 50.47 million HYPE from circulation since the launch of the token. That is 5.05% of the max supply of 1 billion HYPE, and at the current price, the burned tokens are worth around $3.51 billion.

The latest buyback comes amid the decline in the price of the cryptocurrency. On Nov. 12, the price of HYPE went down by 3.45%, and at press time, the token stands at $69.52, according to TradingView. Earlier this autumn, the price of HYPE was above $90.

Hyperliquid pays for the buybacks in dollars, so the decentralized exchange is able to buy more HYPE with the same amount of money when the price of the token goes down.

DeFiLlama data shows that Hyperliquid has earned $1.41 billion for HYPE holders since December 2024. Over the past 30 days, the decentralized exchange earned $68.82 million, and its earnings for the past week stand at $15.04 million.

Over the last day, the platform collected about $1.46 million in fees, slightly less than the amount it spent on the buyback. Besides, DeFiLlama data shows that Hyperliquid currently earns more in fees than Ethereum, Tron and Base.

The largest part of the burned tokens, 48.85 million HYPE, was bought back by the Assistance Fund. The fund automatically receives between 97% and 99% of the fees from perpetual and spot trading on Hyperliquid, and the rest of the fees go to the HLP liquidity pool and to the builders of individual markets.

The Assistance Fund buys HYPE on the open market and sends the tokens to the "0xfefe...fefe" address. It is a system address that does not have a private key. In December 2025, Hyperliquid validators formally recognized the tokens held at this address as burned.

In August 2026, Hyperliquid also introduced the AQAv2 upgrade. Under the upgrade, around 90% of the yield generated by the USDC reserves on the platform now goes to the Assistance Fund. Each of these transfers brings between $14 million and $16 million to the fund.

Besides the Assistance Fund, HYPE is also burned through network fees. Around 1.24 million HYPE has been burned through fees and slashing on HyperCore, the base layer of Hyperliquid. Another 376,060 HYPE has been burned as gas fees on HyperEVM, the smart contract layer of the network.

#5194. Analyst Flags XLM Valuation Gap, Stellar Hits $4B

Home » Altcoins » Analyst Flags XLM Valuation Gap, Stellar Hits $4B

Stellar’s 6.1x growth in the RWA field paints a bright picture for XLM in the revamped financial ecosystem.

Stellar has released a new report from Allium Labs examining institutional activity on its network, showing rapid growth in tokenized real-world assets and stablecoin usage.

The findings arrive as technical analyst ChartNerd noted that XLM “is not priced in” pointing to a disconnect between on-chain institutional progress and the token’s market valuation.

According to the Allium report, Stellar’s tokenized asset market grew 6.1x to $4.12 billion over the 12 months through September 2026, compared with 2.3x growth across the wider market tracked by Allium. This lifted Stellar’s share of all tokenized RWAs from 4.9% to 12.7%.

$XLM is not priced in 🚀 https://t.co/vTs7eBJJGx pic.twitter.com/XfQEwD1O7l

Four new issuers – Spiko, Realiz, Tradable, and Ondo Finance – each now hold more than $500 million, joining Franklin Templeton among the network’s largest issuers. Spiko alone grew its assets on Stellar to $1.69 billion in under a year.

The network has become particularly dominant in one category: it holds 42% of tokenized non-U.S. government debt ($319 million of $766 million outstanding across blockchains as of September 2026), up from just 0.7% a year earlier.

Spiko’s Euro Treasury-bill fund accounts for the large majority of that total.

Stablecoin supply on Stellar rose 91% to $546 million over the same period. Adjusted stablecoin transfer volume reached $1.95 billion over the 12 months through September 2026, up 22%.

Business-to-business payments made up $1.34 billion of that volume (up 26%) and averaged $383 per transaction, far larger than the $15 average for consumer transfers.

The pattern indicates institutions are using the network for higher-value settlement rather than primarily retail activity.

The report includes examples of how established firms operate on the network. Franklin Templeton uses Stellar’s built-in controls to manage holders of its BENJI fund. MoneyGram enables customers to hold digital dollars and withdraw local cash.

A new report from @AlliumLabs digging into institutional finance on Stellar is out now.$4.1B in RWAs (now $5B+.) $319M in non-U.S. debt. 127% growth in stablecoin transfer volume.Plus case studies on @FTDA_US, @MoneyGram, @Spiko_finance, & more.https://t.co/6t2zMFzZ73

Other participants include Spiko and additional issuers that have scaled quickly after launching on the network. Protocol-native features such as holder approvals have been used at scale, with thousands of such actions recorded over the past year.

ChartNerd’s post that “XLM is not priced in” frames the institutional data against XLM’s current market position. The Allium figures show measurable expansion in tokenized assets, non-U.S. debt market share, and business-oriented stablecoin flows.

Whether these on-chain developments eventually narrow any perceived valuation gap depends on continued issuer growth, sustained payment volume, and broader market conditions.

#5193. Israel Discount Bank to Let Customers Trade Bitcoin in App Starting Nov. 26

Israel Discount Bank said Wednesday that from Nov. 26 it will let eligible customers buy, hold and sell bitcoin and ether in the bank’s mobile app, a launch that would beat larger rival Bank Leumi if it stays on schedule.

The Tel Aviv-listed lender described the service as a first for Israel’s banking system in a Nov. 11 statement. Customers will be able to trade the two assets alongside stocks and bonds, without opening an account at an outside exchange or managing a separate digital wallet.

The offer is limited to private, not corporate, customers who are Israeli residents, 18 or older, and whose accounts are already set up for securities trading, subject to the bank’s terms.

“For the first time in the banking system, Discount Bank will allow trading in digital currencies directly from the bank account in a simple and secure manner, without the need for complex dedicated digital wallets,” Avi Levi, CEO of Discount Group, said in the statement. “This is a first step toward establishing advanced technological infrastructure in the digital currency space and developing the bank’s capabilities in this area.”

The rollout was led by Elad Fisher, head of the bank’s financial markets division. Coins will sit in bank-provided wallets built on infrastructure from Fireblocks.

JUST IN: 🇮🇱 $150 billion Israel Discount Bank will become the 1st bank in Israel to let customers buy, hold and sell Bitcoin 👀Adoption! 👏 pic.twitter.com/gwKBy17cSf

The bank’s product page lists a 2% commission on purchases and sales in the app, not including any foreign-exchange conversion. Custody fees are waived for six months from the Nov. 26 launch and then run 0.25% a quarter. Trading is open Sunday through Friday from 9 a.m. to 11 p.m. Israel time, and only immediate orders are allowed. Customers can buy as little as 0.01 of a coin. Holdings appear in the securities portfolio at once, with cash settling the next business day in U.S. dollars; conversion into shekels happens only if the customer asks.

The service is a closed system for now. Bitcoin and ether bought through Discount cannot be sent to an outside wallet, and coins held elsewhere cannot be transferred in. The bank said it may revisit transfers later. It also warns of unusual risk, high volatility and the possibility of a total loss, and says the coins are not legal tender and lack the same protections as securities.

What Discount is selling, besides convenience, is tax treatment. Capital gains and losses on the coins can be offset automatically against the rest of the customer’s investment portfolio for tax withheld at source, the bank said, at a 25% rate on profits. Sale proceeds land directly in the customer’s bank account, without the source-of-funds questionnaires that have often accompanied money coming back from crypto platforms.

Discount is not the first Israeli bank to promise bitcoin trading. Bank Leumi, the country’s largest lender, announced a plan as early as March 2022 to offer bitcoin and ether through Pepper Invest with Paxos, a project that did not reach a full-scale launch. In August, Leumi and Galaxy Digital (GLXY) said Leumi and Pepper customers would be able to buy, hold and sell bitcoin, ether and Solana in the Leumi Trade app, with a launch expected in early 2027.

That announcement billed Leumi as the first bank in Israel to offer the service. If Discount opens on Nov. 26, it would get there first, though Leumi still plans to offer Solana, which Discount is not including at the start. Discount said it may later consider adding other assets.

In an Oct. 7 circular, the Bank of Israel’s Banking Supervision Department amended Directive 411 to say that a bank may not refuse payment services solely because the funds come from virtual-currency activity, a change that takes effect May 1, 2027. The amendment does not authorize banks to offer crypto trading, but it is part of a broader effort, first floated in a July draft, to widen how banks handle crypto-related payments.

The test now is whether Discount hits the Nov. 26 date, and whether customers accept a closed wallet and a 2% commission in exchange for bank custody and automatic tax withholding. Leumi still has early 2027 to catch up.

#5192. Nvidia Erases 2026 Gains as Republicans Threaten Jensen Huang With Subpoena

Nvidia Erases 2026 Gains as Republicans Threaten Jensen Huang With Subpoena

Nvidia shares plunged to $166.95 Tuesday afternoon, leaving the AI chipmaker down 18.42% for November and wiping out all the gains it had accumulated in 2026.

The selloff accelerated as congressional Republicans threatened to subpoena CEO Jensen Huang over his reported relationship with a suspected Chinese intelligence operative.

At that price, Nvidia was roughly 10.5% below its $186.50 closing price at the end of 2025 and 31.4% below its recent $243.37 peak. November is shaping up as its worst month since September 2022, when shares lost roughly 19.6%; the company suffered an even deeper 32% crash in April of that year.

Tuesday's drop exceeded 6% in afternoon trading, after a 4.3% fall on Monday. The two-day decline was on track to erase more than $470 billion in market value. Even after the significant drop, some analysts would still view the stock as extremely overbought in the long term, with prices much higher than what would be considered safe based on technical indicators.

Just for context, even an additional 20% crash (-43% from October) would be considered healthy in an isolated technical analysis, leaving the price still above the average price of the last 50 months. Prices tend to either bounce or trade near that line. When prices go too far away from the average, that usually means there is an unusual appetite for the asset, typically driven more by FOMO than fundamentals.

House Foreign Affairs Committee Chairman Brian Mast told ABC News on Tuesday that he was prepared to subpoena Huang unless the CEO responded by Wednesday, November 11, to a request for a closed-door briefing. No subpoena had been issued at the time of the report.

Mast and two other Republican committee chairs asked Huang to appear on November 18 and explain his interactions with Alice Huang, a former executive at Singapore-based chip buyer Megaspeed. The lawmakers also want details about Nvidia's safeguards against foreign espionage, according to a letter reviewed by Reuters.

U.S. authorities have reportedly investigated whether Alice Huang, who has no family relationship to the Nvidia CEO, passed information to Beijing. The allegations have not established that Jensen Huang knowingly disclosed confidential information or violated export rules.

On Sunday, Nvidia's board announced an independent investigation into whether its chief executive was targeted by a foreign intelligence operation. The board said it had no reason to believe that national security or company secrets were compromised, but would examine the available information.

Earlier today, the Financial Times reported Nvidia has hired former U.S. prosecutor Joon H Kim to conduct the internal investigation.

The company is also facing legal pressure. Shareholders filed a proposed class-action lawsuit Monday alleging that Nvidia and Huang failed to disclose risks associated with the reported relationship.

The scandal has landed during a broader reassessment of the AI boom. Investors have questioned how long massive spending on data centers can continue, and Nvidia's competitors are offering customers more alternatives to its expensive chips.

Google introduced new AI chip configurations Monday. The Information reported that OpenAI was considering buying Google's tensor processing units, or TPUs, which are processors built specifically to run AI workloads. Neither company has confirmed a purchase but yesterday Google announced a new line of TPUs available for select customers, directly competing against Nvidia’s chips.

Nvidia is scheduled to report quarterly earnings on November 17, one day before the congressional briefing requested by House Republicans. Mast's deadline for Huang to respond comes first, on November 11.

#5191. MSCI Drops Saylor's Strategy From Its Index Amid BTC Sell-Off

MSCI has published the results of its November 2026 Index Review, with Strategy among the companies set to leave the MSCI USA Index.

Metaplanet, the Tokyo-listed Bitcoin treasury company, is also scheduled for removal from the MSCI Japan Small Cap Index.

The changes will take effect after the market close on November 30, 2026.

Across the MSCI ACWI Index, 13 securities will be added and 100 deleted. The two additions to the MSCI World Index, measured by full company market capitalization, are Moderna (USA) and Aritzia (Canada).

The latest review follows the new rules proposed by MSCI in October, among which are new measures regarding crypto treasury holding companies.

MSCI evaluates whether a company maintains substantial operating assets and applies five financial ratio tests. In case a company fails to meet at least four criteria,

A company that fails at least four criteria across consecutive annual reporting periods may face removal from the MSCI Global Investable Market Indexes.

MSCI indexes track public companies based on market cap and investment risks. Global mutual funds and ETFs use these benchmarks to build their portfolios.

Dropping out of such index could influence investment flows as well as stock prices.

Strategy continues to accumulate BTC despite the crypto market staying in red zone.

Between November 2 and November 8, 2026, the company acquired another 1,036 BTC at an average price of $79,012.

As of November 12, Strategy holds 850,364 BTC. The company also reported about $5.5 billion in USD reserves.

However, Bitcoin's recent decline below $75,000 pushes Strategy's holdings into an unrealized loss of $500 million.

Strategy's stock (MSTR) fell 7% on Thursday, while Bitcoin is down 3.5% at the time of writing.

#5190. Everyone’s selling XRP right now, but why?

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

XRP [XRP] has been sliding on the charts for weeks, and it looks like everyone’s selling their stash right now.

Whales have been steady though. They’ve been trading big, but we don’t quite know whose side they’re on.

Cryptoquant’s Futures Taker CVD has been sell-dominant since late August. That hasn’t changed yet so, two and a half months straight.

The only time this year Futures traders leaned buy-heavy was in the beginning of 2026. In that time, XRP has gone from $1.55 in late September to $1.05 now.

Spot traders used to turn a blind eye, but not anymore. For the most part, their CVD was neutral this year. There were some buy-heavy stretches in January, February and May.

That changed in October. Now, the Spot Take CVD is also sell-dominant. This is the first time that’s happened all year!

That matters more than you think. Futures selling might just be traders betting short. When Spot starts selling too, that would mean that people holding actual XRP may be getting out.

Usually, when people trade a coin this much, it’s because the price is rising. With XRP though, that’s not been the case.

Futures volume bubble map went red in late August. Through most of September and early October, it was marked as “overheating.” While that’s slowed down a bit since, it’s still been a fair spike.

It moved into “heating” in early September, was “overheating” in the middle of the month, and has stayed hot ever since.

Now, put that next to when Spot volume was “cooling” at $1.40.

Both markets are busy right now, but since it’s all during a dip, it may be indicative of selling.

Finally, CryptoQuant’s Average Order Size has shown “big whale orders” on Futures and Spot since early October.

That’s happened before as well. Whale-sized orders were all the rage through June, July, and most of August. All this, while XRP went between $1 and $1.25.

Towards the end of August, the price went back to $1.50 on the charts.

As with many others, this metric also only shows how big orders are, and not which way they’re going. Whales could be buying the dip, or walking away.

Either way, they’re the ones moving the most money right now. Whatever they decide at $1 is probably where XRP goes next.

#5189. Binance Says It Has Fully Covered the $6.34 Billion Hack With Company Funds

Binance said Thursday it has completed coverage of the $6.34 billion Oct. 29 hack from company funds, two weeks after the largest crypto theft on record, even as withdrawals continue to reopen in phases.

In a Thursday announcement, the exchange wrote that it “has completed coverage of assets impacted in the security incident” and published a new proof-of-reserves snapshot. It repeated the same message on X.

That wording matters. Coverage means Binance has replaced the affected tokens from its own books, not that it has recovered the stolen coins from the attackers. The exchange said it committed more than $6.34 billion in company funds and bought the affected tokens through market and over-the-counter channels, drawing on a partial deployment of its Secure Asset Fund for Users (SAFU), a portion of its futures insurance fund above the level required under its risk-management framework, and its corporate treasury.

Binance has also committed to replenishing SAFU to about $1 billion after that partial draw, a step it said would keep the fund fully resourced as an emergency user-protection reserve.

A very small number of tokens the exchange no longer lists were also affected. Binance said those holdings will be handled under its delisting process and that users who hold them will be covered.

Binance took a new proof-of-reserves snapshot at 00:00 UTC on Nov. 12, or 7 p.m. Eastern on Wednesday, covering 198 tokens. That is a sharp jump from the 10 tokens included in the Oct. 31 snapshot published in the days after the breach. The new figures are now posted on the exchange’s proof-of-reserves website.

The snapshot, Binance said, “provides an updated view of assets that are fully accounted for.” Users cannot yet check that claim themselves. The exchange said the full zero-knowledge proof will take about 40 to 50 hours to generate because of the computational workload. After that, users holding the affected tokens “will be able to independently verify that their assets are backed at a minimum of 1:1.”

Until the proof is ready, the 1:1 backing is Binance’s statement, not something customers can confirm on their own.

Thursday’s update does not reopen the platform all at once. Binance said it continues to reopen withdrawals in phases and will share further details once they are confirmed. The exchange first reopened withdrawals on Nov. 1, starting with bitcoin and then expanding to other networks, and has kept the restart on a phased timetable since then.

The announcement also does not say how much of the stolen assets have been retrieved, and it does not name an attacker. Covering customer balances leaves that recovery question unanswered. The theft, which Binance first detailed in a Nov. 3 incident report, began with a fake job interview and a poisoned developer tool and drained hot wallets on Oct. 29, in what remains the largest crypto hack on record.

The exchange had already pledged more than $6.34 billion to cover customer losses in the hours after the breach, reported coverage of 90% and then 95% as it reopened withdrawals, and later said it had replenished 98% of affected tokens, with the remainder in less liquid assets that took longer to buy. Thursday’s statement is the first time Binance has said that process is complete.

Binance also said security remains a priority and that work on its infrastructure is still underway. For customers, the immediate tests are simpler: whether the zero-knowledge proof, due in about two days, backs the new snapshot, and how quickly the rest of the withdrawal queues come back.

#5187. XRP, SHIB, BTC and Other Tokens Hit Hard by Abrupt Resignation of Nvidia CEO

The unexpected exit of the Nvidia co-founder has created pandemonium in the financial world as shares of the artificial intelligence company have been hit by a heavy sell-off, further straining an already weak crypto market.

According to reports by Reuters, Nvidia chief executive Jensen Huang resigned on Nov. 12. Chief Financial Officer Colette Kress has taken over as interim chief executive.

There are controversies surrounding Huang's personal life and doubts about his future at the company he co-founded over three decades ago.

The news of Huang's exit has triggered a heavy sell-off in Nvidia stock, with shares dropping over 12% in early trading, wiping out an estimated $500 billion in market value.

The sell-off has also affected crypto-related trading markets.

Meanwhile, the crypto market itself is reeling from heavy losses as some top altcoins are faring worse than Bitcoin.

The recent crypto market data shows that Bitcoin (BTC) is currently trading around $71,323, shedding about 3% in the last 24 hours.

Over the last seven days, the largest cryptocurrency by market cap has lost even more, shedding about 10.2%.

However, XRP has seen a more pronounced sell-off.

#5186. Trump Media Posts $410 Million Profit. Bitcoin Gains Did the Heavy Lifting

Trump Media Posts $410 Million Profit. Bitcoin Gains Did the Heavy Lifting

Trump Media & Technology Group reported a $409.7 million third-quarter profit on Monday, a sharp turnaround for the company behind President Donald Trump's Truth Social.

However, most of the money that Trump Media & Technology made didn't come from media or technology. It came from the rising value of its crypto holdings and related investments.

The company generated just $2.97 million in revenue during the three months ended September 30, according to its SEC filing. A year earlier, it had reported a $54.8 million loss.

Trump Media recorded $496.1 million in realized and unrealized gains on digital assets, pledged crypto and equity securities, according to its earnings release. Much of that was an accounting gain—assets rose in value even when the company hadn't sold them for cash.

Digital assets and pledged coins accounted for $378.1 million of those gains, with securities adding another $118 million. Derivative losses and other expenses offset part of the windfall.

Bitcoin supplied a major boost. Chief Financial Officer Phillip Juhan said on the earnings call that it rose about 42% during the quarter, from roughly $59,000 at the end of June to $84,000 at the end of September.

Trump Media held approximately 15,649 Bitcoin at quarter-end, including around 11,385 coins pledged to a program designed to generate additional returns. Some of those assets are subject to arrangements allowing the counterparty to reuse the coins, according to the filing.

The company expanded into crypto in July 2025, purchasing about $2 billion in Bitcoin and related securities. That decision has tied its earnings closely to changes in digital-asset prices.

The downside was obvious just three months ago. Back in August Trump Media lost $238 million in the second quarter as declining crypto prices battered its holdings. This quarter, the accounting effect went in the opposite direction.

Truth Social's underlying business improved, though from a small base. Revenue increased 205% from about $973,000 a year earlier, supported by subscriptions for Truth+ streaming and Truth API, a paid service that gives customers faster access to posts on the platform that critics have likened to insider trading.

The company also recorded growth in advertising sales and fees from its Truth.Fi funds. Even so, Trump Media reported a $12.3 million adjusted EBITDA loss, a measure that excludes investment gains and certain other items, compared with a $29.1 million loss a year earlier.

The latest quarterly profit has not repaired the longer-term picture for shareholders. DJT shares traded around $4.20 Tuesday, up about 3.3% on the day but down 94.7% from their $79.38 registered back in 2024. The stock has lost nearly 70% this year.

Trump Media still posted a $234.2 million net loss for the first nine months of 2026. It is also pursuing a $6 billion merger with fusion-energy developer TAE Technologies, which shareholders are scheduled to vote on December 14.

Before that vote, holders of the company's $1 billion in convertible notes can demand cash repayment on November 30, according to its quarterly filing.

#5185. Here’s why Zcash could remove 50,000 ZEC from its grants treasury

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

The Zcash [ZEC] community is gearing up to vote on a proposal that could significantly change how ZEC funds its development and distributes newly created tokens. The plan involves removing 50,000 ZEC from the Zcash Community Grants (ZCG) treasury and redirecting its value toward future mining rewards.

That said, Zcash currently allocates 8% of newly issued ZEC to ZCG, which uses the funds to support developers, wallet providers, and other ecosystem projects.

These grants help maintain the infrastructure and software needed to use ZEC. However, rising ZEC prices has increased the value of ZCG’s existing treasury, which has raised concerns that the committee was accumulating more funds than necessary while continuing to receive new token allocations.

However, now, under the new proposal, approximately 55% of ZCG’s treasury (50,000 ZEC) would be removed from circulation within 30 days of activation. Going ahead, its value would be credited to the Network Sustainability Mechanism, introduced through the NU7 upgrade. This, in turn, would gradually return ZEC to the network through future mining rewards.

At the same time, the proposal would also end ZCG’s existing 8% allocation of newly created ZEC. Instead, this share would accumulate in a new Future Dev and Legal Defense Fund until the next halving takes place.

As this happens, the fund would remain inaccessible until a separate proposal establishes withdrawal rules. Lastly, ZCG would retain its remaining treasury to continue funding development, while the separate 12% Coinholder-Controlled Fund would remain unchanged.

For those unaware, the revised plan follows ZCG’s 3rd November decision to abandon an earlier proposal, which faced criticism for seeking to redirect approximately 98,000 ZEC from existing assets and future income toward mining rewards.

So, now, the next step is community voting, which is scheduled from 16th November to 30th November. Coinholders and community panels will assess the proposal’s support.

If it receives rough consensus, developers, and miners can work toward implementing it through a future network upgrade called NU7.1. However, there is no confirmed activation date yet.

This coincided with Zcash’s monthly active developer count having nearly doubled over the past two years.

According to Glassnode’s report, around 70% of its active developers have more than one year of experience in crypto development, compared with 58% across the wider industry.

Still, ZEC’s price action was under bearish pressure as it was trading at $986.40 at press time after a drop of 1.4% in the past 24 hours.

This followed a recent report by AMBCrypto wherein ZEC fell by 15.25% and Open Interest also declined 13.4%, indicating weakening speculative demand.

#5184. 'Yes': SBI CEO Yoshitaka Kitao Answers XRP Holder Doubts in One Word

Disclaimer: The opinions expressed by our writers are their own and do not represent the views of U.Today. The financial and market information provided on U.Today is intended for informational purposes only. U.Today is not liable for any financial losses incurred while trading cryptocurrencies. Conduct your own research by contacting financial experts before making any investment decisions. We believe that all content is accurate as of the date of publication, but certain offers mentioned may no longer be available.

Yoshitaka Kitao, head of Japanese financial group SBI Holdings, answered "YES" when asked point-blank whether he still trusts XRP.

The question came from an X user. The user wrote that the past few weeks had been "truly exhausting" for XRP holders, and said that token holders feel that Ripple has left them behind.

Kitao answered in English. Most of his recent posts on X are in Japanese, and the rest of them are reposts of other accounts.

Kitao previously worked as an executive at Nomura Securities and later served as CFO of SoftBank. In 1999, he founded SoftBank Investment, which later became SBI Holdings. Today, SBI includes banks, brokerage, and insurance businesses, as well as crypto services.

The partnership began in 2016. SBI joined Ripple's Series B round and launched a joint venture with Ripple, SBI Ripple Asia. SBI owns about 60% of the venture, and Ripple owns the remaining 40%.

The same year, SBI Ripple Asia set up a consortium of Japanese banks to test Ripple's technology for payments, and the consortium later grew to more than 60 banks.

In 2018, SBI launched the interbank app MoneyTap. The same year, SBI's crypto exchange, now called SBI VC Trade, started operations with XRP trading. In 2019, Kitao joined Ripple's board of directors.

From 2020, SBI began handing the token out to shareholders as bonuses, and the payment service SBI Remit launched remittances using it with on-demand liquidity (ODL), including in the major Japan-Philippines corridor.

SBI kept working with Ripple during the SEC lawsuit against the company, which lasted from December 2020 until 2025.

Back on February 15, 2026, in rare long-form tweets in English, Kitao denied rumors that SBI holds $10 billion worth of XRP: "Not $10 billion in XRP, but around 9% of Ripple Labs. So our hidden asset could be much bigger." By his account, the combined valuation of Ripple Labs and its ecosystem is "enormous," and SBI owns more than 9% of all of it.

Kitao also talked about XRP at SBI's quarterly briefing on November 2, 2026. He said that investors buy Bitcoin mostly to hold it, and that the value of XRP depends on its use in payments and business ecosystems.

In August 2025, SBI VC Trade and Ripple agreed to bring the RLUSD stablecoin to Japan. In October 2025, SBI also invested $200 million in Evernorth, a company that holds XRP in its treasury. By now, SBI VC Trade has more than two million accounts.

#5183. Simon Dixon: What It Would Take for Me to Sell My Bitcoin for Gold

Simon Dixon, CEO and co-founder of BnkToTheFuture, tells BMTV there are only three things that would make him sell all of his Bitcoin and buy gold.

Simon Dixon, CEO and co-founder of BnkToTheFuture, tells BMTV there are only three things that would make him sell all of his Bitcoin and buy gold. Since 2011, he says Bitcoin has done three things incredibly well: you can earn and hold it in self-custody, spend it peer-to-peer without a government in the middle, and rely on a fixed supply of 21 million as a hedge against long-term debasement. If any of those three is ever called into question, he would sell immediately. The real battle, in his view, is not whether Bitcoin gets adopted — he says institutional adoption is on a trajectory we have never seen — but whether custodians, Wall Street-owned miners, and treasury companies end up centralizing the network.

CryptoQuant’s analyst Dark Frost has dug into the historical data behind the shift, witnessing a similar structure just two months before XRP hit $3.55 back in 2025. The rolling monthly figure is particularly important to swing traders looking for the price bottom, below all 4 moving averages.

#5181. How Ethereum staking hit a record 36.3% despite ETH’s price drop

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Ethereum’s [ETH] staking ratio has reportedly reached an all-time high (ATH)  of 36.3%, accounting for more than one-third of ETH’s total circulating supply. Well, the rising staking ratio suggests that an increasing number of ETH holders are choosing to lock up their tokens rather than sell or actively trade them.

This comes as Ethereum’s validator queues show that at press time, 911,938 ETH was waiting to be staked with a 16-day, 7-hour wait. Meanwhile, 264,211 ETH was queued to exit with a 5-day, 16-hour wait, plus a 7-day sweep delay.

Both queues are limited by the same churn rate of 256 per epoch, meaning only a fixed amount of ETH can join or leave each epoch, which is why a big backlog translates into longer waits.

In other words, more people want to stake than unstake right now, but the entry line is slowly shrinking, and exiting takes roughly 12-13 days in total once the sweep delay is included.

This comes as the total ETH staked rose from about 42.1 million ETH (roughly 34.5% of supply) in mid-August to about 44.5 million ETH (around 36.5%) at press time. Since late October the climb has resumed steadily.

Of this, BitMine earned $121.4 million from staking and validation activities, accounting for approximately 97% of its total revenue. By the 8th of November, the largest Ethereum DAT held 6.07 million ETH, of which 5.22 million ETH was staked.

That said, the company also estimates that these holdings could generate around $309 million in annual staking rewards, based on an estimated 2.65% yield and an ETH price of $2,234.

On the other hand, SharpLink has accumulated 31,363 ETH in staking rewards as of 9th November. Its strategy also includes liquid staking through Lido, allowing it to receive wstETH in return for its deposited ETH.

All of this happened while the active validators moved in the opposite direction. As per charts, they held near 900K through mid-September, peaking around 912K. But again, they dropped sharply from mid-September to mid-October, falling to about 815K, and have since drifted down to roughly 807K, with a small additional drop on the last day or two.

However, the price action of ETH trading at $2,042.94 at press time after a modest drop in the past 24 hours but a 15.5% drop in the past week sparks concern. This comes as Ethereum faces renewed selling pressure after losing its $2,250 support level. However, Vitalik Buterin’s recent push to position ETH as a store of value suggests hope.

#5179. Bitcoin Miner HIVE Eyes $45 Million in Annual Revenue From AI Deal

Bitcoin Miner HIVE Eyes $45 Million in Annual Revenue From AI Deal

Bitcoin miner HIVE Digital Technologies is eyeing $45 million in annual revenue from an AI data center in Sweden, but it still hasn't secured a final lease.

In its latest earnings update, HIVE reiterated that its 32-megawatt Big Boden facility could generate the sum through a long-term colocation arrangement. That revenue depends on a definitive agreement, financing, facility upgrades, and the project entering service.

The company first disclosed the proposal in June. It involves an unnamed, investment-grade Swedish technology company and contemplates a lease lasting up to 10 years. During Tuesday's earnings call, CEO Aydin Kilic said the parties were negotiating deposits, termination rights, and other terms that could affect financing costs. A lender's consultant had visited the site the previous week, he said.

“We do not want to rush into a deal just for the sake of reporting new top-line revenue,” Kilic said, according to the earnings call transcript.

HIVE expects to seek financing for the Boden project after securing a signed lease, potentially through corporate bonds. The company has not announced a completion date for negotiations.

The existing data center would also need a substantial makeover. HIVE plans to retrofit it for up to 10,000 Nvidia GB300 GPUs, using liquid cooling to handle high-density computing racks.

Although the facility has a 32-megawatt utility connection, the proposed customer would receive approximately 25 megawatts of usable computing capacity, with the remaining power supporting data center operations.

“This site has evolved from a GPU compute facility supporting Ethereum,” Kilic said when announcing the letter of intent, describing its planned conversion into a more powerful AI installation.

The expansion follows HIVE's broader effort to move beyond Bitcoin mining, a shift that has already produced several large GPU-cloud contracts.

HIVE signed a major three-year AI computing agreement with Bell Canada and Cohere in June, covering 2,304 Nvidia GPUs as it expanded beyond crypto mining.

HIVE now reports $185 million in contracted GPU-cloud annualized run-rate revenue, although only $40 million is live. The remainder represents contracted capacity that has not yet entered service.

The company is targeting approximately $700 million in total high-performance computing annualized revenue by the end of 2028, though that figure includes projects requiring additional financing, construction, and customer commitments.

For Boden, HIVE's next milestone is securing a definitive lease before proceeding with project financing.

#5178. Russia Legalizes Crypto Trading While XRP Stands Apart

Home » Bitcoin (BTC) » Russia Legalizes Crypto Trading While XRP Stands Apart

Platforms are able to conduct business operations in crypto, offering retail knowledge-based accessibility.

Russia’s crypto bid is going at a faster pace since the fourth largest economy around the globe introduced crypto trading for retail & businesses. The new set of rules, coming from the Central Bank Of Russia, limits regular investors to a 300,000 rubles yearly cap (roughly $3,600).

This allows buying & holding Bitcoin (BTC), Ethereum (ETH) & USDT through regulated intermediaries (brokers, trust managers & licensed exchanges). Everyone must pass a knowledge test plus review risk disclosures before starting trading activities, while XRP is considered an asset for advanced users only.

The Moscow Exchange already lists cash-settled perpetual futures (XRP/USDF) and related indices tracking XRP prices. These are available only to qualified investors, settle in rubles (no actual XRP is delivered), and form part of a broader set of crypto derivatives with substantial turnover among Futures traders across the scene.

Russian Central Bank: Exchanges May Conduct Crypto Trading from November 22, Subject to Prior Approval of Trading Rules According to TASS, the Bank of Russia said Russian exchanges may begin offering cryptocurrency trading from November 22, 2026, provided they first agree on… pic.twitter.com/txoYyq5vfL

The fresh legal framework dates back to August. Then, Russia’s President Vladimir Putin signed the inaugural comprehensive cryptocurrency law (Federal Law No. 282-FZ, “On Digital Currencies and Digital Rights”), setting September 1, 2026 activation window: contrastingly to the entry-level investor restrictions, qualified ones have no limits.

Meanwhile, businesses face hard limits, needing to meet a minimum equity requirement (15 million rubles, roughly $187,000) to run crypto services or custody services. The regulatory framework is way more lenient on businesses conducting cross-border sanctions to dodge the 21-chapter European Union sanction packages.

The retail crypto trading gets legalized on November 22, 2026, the related Duma documents state. The approved exchanges & crypto brokers are obliged to report the trading data and results to the central bank in accordance with existing reporting procedures for XRP & others.

For XRP traders in the region, the MOEX XRP Futures index allows up to 5x leverage.

RUSSIA MADE A MASSIVE $XRP MOVE!! pic.twitter.com/dmuUaPnsuP

Paying with XRP or any other popular crypto coin at a local store is still off the table. Goods & services are prohibited as a result of a separate ban, pushing crypto adoption in the perspective of an investment vehicle rather than a means of payment.

Still, setting up state-surveilled crypto brokers is a pretty big move that could inspire other major economies to do the same.

#5177. Peter Schiff on the Bitcoin Crash: “Get the Hell Out”

Challenged on why two or three down years would matter for Bitcoin when gold and the Nasdaq have had similar streaks before recoveries, Peter Schiff says the difference is the backdrop.

Challenged on why two or three down years would matter for Bitcoin when gold and the Nasdaq have had similar streaks before recoveries, Peter Schiff says the difference is the backdrop. When a market can’t go up on the best news it has ever had, he argues, that normally indicates that it’s going to go down. He calls Bitcoin “all hype,” with no intrinsic value, income, or dividends, and says much of the supply now sits with ETF investors and Bitcoin treasury companies rather than people who want to use it as money. All of that hot money, he warns, is going to exit, the market will go down faster than it went up, and he expects a massive washout. Even long-term bulls, he says, will have cheaper opportunities after leveraged speculators are cleared out, and he adds that this is not the time to be hodling.

#5176. Can TRX/ETH extend its 23% rally after Tron takes the lead with USDT?

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

Read the best crypto stories of the day in less than 5 minutes. Join to get it daily in your inbox.

A key shift is taking shape in the market, and it is one traders should keep a close eye on.

On a macro level, the stablecoin market is currently at one of its lowest levels since the 2022 bear market. The total supply of Tether (USDT) has fallen by almost $15 billion from its peak of $310 billion in mid-May. This indicated that liquidity has been flowing out of the crypto market, which is why demand has been pretty low even though Bitcoin is now trading almost 30% off its $126K high.

As the largest stablecoin hub, Ethereum has taken a significant hit too.

According to DeFiLlama, the total amount of stablecoins on Ethereum fell by over $20 billion. This was compounded by Tether as in the last 30 days alone, the total supply of USDT on Ethereum fell by nearly 9% to just over $66 billion. The key takeaway, however, may be Tron’s USDT supply.

As shown in the chart above, USDT supply on the Tron blockchain was down by only just over 1% at press time. This was much lower than the 9% decrease seen on Ethereum. This hinted at a clear divergence in stablecoin liquidity between the two networks.

With Ethereum seeing heavier USDT outflows, Tron’s share of total USDT supply climbed above 52%, reinforcing its position as the dominant network for USDT liquidity.

Interestingly, Tron founder Justin Sun also pointed out this trend in a recent X post, highlighting data from Token Terminal. In the past 12 months, TRON recorded a $16.1 billion hike in stablecoin supply, the highest among 45 tracked blockchains. HyperEVM came second. Arc also had an increase of $1.3 billion while Arbitrum decreased by $4.3 billion. On the contrary, Ethereum saw a more significant $30.6 billion decline.

In short, Tron’s [TRX] stablecoin dominance may be taking down Ethereum. It is also making inroads into other major Layer 1 networks. According to AMBCrypto, with increasing liquidity advantages, TRX is an altcoin to watch when the market moves back into a risk-on environment.

The real impact of this divergence becomes clearer when looking at on-chain activity.

Interestingly, data from the Tron network also seemed to confirm the stablecoins trend. Within a 24-hour period, Tron processed 12,990,075 transactions. This is the highest daily total for the past 7 days, averaging at around 150 transactions per second (TPS). This suggested that there may be consistent demand on the network, with the increase in stablecoins on Tron being due to real demand.

The result? The TRX/ETH ratio has risen by over 23% so far in Q4 and was only 15% away from making up all of its Q3 losses. On top of that, with stablecoin liquidity and on-chain activity between the two projects increasing, this trend may be accelerating.

If this continues, a break above the 0.002-resistance level could become more likely, opening the door for further TRX outperformance against ETH.

Taken together, with increasing stablecoin dominance, high volume, and the 23% rally in the TRX/ETH ratio, 0.002 is a key level. If it breaks through it could be a testement to TRX’s strength, while a failure to clear resistance may leave the pair trading within its current range.

As it stands, the combination of stablecoin inflows and growing network activity means that TRON is well-positioned to outperform Ethereum [ETH] this quarter.

Tron gained significant stablecoin share as Ethereum saw heavier outflows and weaker on-chain activity.

At press time, TRX/ETH was up 23% in Q4, with 0.002 as the next key resistance level.

#5175. Ripple's RLUSD Overtakes PayPal's PYUSD

Ripple's US dollar-backed stablecoin RLUSD has set a new record by surpassing PayPal USD (PYUSD) in market capitalization to cement its position as a top stablecoin globally.

Recent data from CoinGecko shows that RLUSD's market cap has grown to around $2.71 billion, enabling it to surpass its competitor, PayPal's stablecoin, with a market cap of around $2.69 billion.

This milestone was achieved less than two years after Ripple's formal introduction of RLUSD in December 2024, underscoring its rapid growth in the crypto market.

A prominent XRP Ledger validator, Vet, also commented on this development to underscore the increasing significance of RLUSD in the stablecoin industry.

"RLUSD is now a Top 5 USD backed stablecoin. On its way to be in Top 3," Vet commented.

Vet noted Ripple's long-standing contribution to building the XRP Ledger. He added that the increasing adoption of RLUSD could potentially lead to faster adoption of the blockchain by traditional financial institutions.

Vet stressed the increasing relevance of RLUSD's rising payment volume and increased use of the stablecoin on the XRP Ledger.

He also noted that RLUSD has opened the door to numerous integrations with banks and crypto exchanges.

The XRP Ledger validator further noted that these integrations could motivate financial institutions to not only run infrastructure to support the XRPL but also integrate other tokenized assets on it.

This new record for RLUSD comes on the back of several key institutional partnerships and exchange integrations for RLUSD that Ripple has been announcing recently.

On Oct. 28, Ripple announced a partnership with Coinbase to provide RLUSD for retail and institutional users across Coinbase Exchange, Coinbase Prime and its retail trading app.

As part of the partnership, Coinbase also announced support for RLUSD across different blockchain networks such as the XRP Ledger, Ethereum and Base.

On the other hand, Ripple's partnership with banking giant BNY, which is the custodian of reserves for RLUSD, is also deepening.

Besides this, Ripple has also managed to secure support from Standard Chartered, which launched institutional custody services for RLUSD on the XRP Ledger back in October.

#5174. Prisoner Jailed for 8 Years Over $2.7M Crypto Theft Plot Run From His Cell

Prisoner Jailed for 8 Years Over $2.7M Crypto Theft Plot Run From His Cell

A man who directed an attempted £2 million ($2.7 million) cryptocurrency theft from his prison cell has been jailed for eight years by Scotland's High Court of Justiciary.

Robert Barr, 29, pleaded guilty along with Sean Favier, 26, and Iain Doyle, 37, to being involved in serious organised crime as part of the attempted theft, according to a sentencing statement published by Scotland's Judicial Office on Tuesday. Lord Cubie sentenced Favier to four years and Doyle to five years at the High Court in Glasgow.

"This was a crime which married 21st century computer sophistication with old fashioned housebreaking in an audacious attempt to steal cryptocurrency worth up to £2 million pounds," Lord Cubie said.

The plan, according to the court's account, was for Favier and Doyle to get into the victims' home in Midlothian, near Edinburgh, and make the man go onto his computer and download AnyDesk, a remote-access program. That would let Barr, who was serving a 21-month sentence at HMP Edinburgh for fraud, take control of the computer from prison and transfer the crypto to wallets he controlled.

The two men went to the house at around 11:30 p.m. on April 17, 2024, but the couple saw them on their doorbell camera and refused to let them in. One of the men looked into the lens and asked whether there was a camera in front of him before the pair left, the BBC reported.

The attempt followed months of preparation, including identifying the victim as a crypto holder, gathering images of the house and reconnaissance, all of which investigators pieced together from Barr's phone after it was seized in June 2024, the judge said. Messages on the phone "plainly contemplated threats and/or violence," he added.

The victim was a co-founder of a crypto startup who did not advertise his holdings, but followed mostly crypto-related accounts on social media, according to the BBC. Barr's associates had already burgled the house in January 2024 and taken a crypto wallet, but could not access the funds without its PIN, the court heard.

Prosecutors described Barr as a "sophisticated cybercriminal" able to "orchestrate a level of criminality rarely seen within mainstream investigations." He had a previous conviction for a crypto fraud, and organised the new offence while in prison "using plainly illicit means of being in contact or online," Lord Cubie said, calling those materially aggravating factors.

The judge said that the plot was only frustrated because the victims "had the sense not to open their front door" to unknown men with their faces covered. Lord Cubie will consider a serious crime prevention order against Barr on November 17. Barr is also in custody awaiting potential extradition, the judge noted.

Had the men got inside, the plot would likely have escalated into a so-called wrench attack, in which criminals use force or the threat of it to make crypto holders hand over their funds. Such attacks are on pace for a record year, with more than $30 million stolen in the first half of 2026, according to a Chainalysis report.

The UK has seen several recent cases. Three teenagers were jailed earlier this year after stealing $4.3 million in crypto at knifepoint, and three men were sentenced last month over a failed home invasion aimed at stealing crypto. In September, Crimestoppers offered a reward of up to £10,000 for information on an attack on a UK couple in which, police believe, a fourth man relayed instructions to the three attackers over a video call.

#5173. Binance Halts Luna Classic Burns: What’s Going On?

Home » Altcoins » Binance Halts Luna Classic Burns: What’s Going On?

Terra Luna Classic’s community got used to the monthly burning ceremonies, but this time it’s postponed.

A few weeks after Binance’s $6.4 billion hack following an employee’s laptop breach, the largest digital asset exchange across the globe is resuming most of their regular services. However, the Terra Luna Classic (LUNC) community has missed the monthly LUNC burn, coming from 50% of fees garnered from Binance’s LUNC-related trading pairs.

Typically done on the first of the month, this time Binance’s LUNC burn didn’t arrive at all, chain records show. That’s the first break in a pattern running since March 2023, when Binance pledged to help restore the downtrodden Terra Luna Classic community’s hope in LUNC’s long-term restoration efforts via continuous token burns.

On-chain records show 50 batches of LUNC burns being carried out by Binance on an earlier note. The last one, which was on October 1, 2026, eliminated 335 million tokens, pushing the exchange’s cumulative Terra Luna Classic burn count towards 91 billion.

That’s roughly 19.6% of all LUNC burns to this date, a considerable contribution in the deflationary campaign.

Still no statement from @Binance regarding the monthly #LUNC burn.. Although we understand the massive hack, I feel as though a statement should at least be made to #LunaClassic investors. Is this a temporary pause or indefinite?.. pic.twitter.com/Od2yH1kFgn

Some LUNC community members are floating the main reason for the delay to be apparent changes in wallet security, but Binance confirmed no such action. Naturally, the internal wallet reshuffling can be a normal practice following the multi-billion dollar hack and the day-by-day withdrawal restoration efforts done with diligence.

Back in August, the community behind Terra Luna Classic revisited the proposal 12223 to raise the on-chain transaction tax to 1.5%. Of that, 1.2 percentage points go towards burning even more LUNC coins. Meanwhile, strong voices in the crypto community have been advocating for more exchanges beyond Binance to burn fixed-fee allocations.

KuCoin & OKX were among the ones named in negotiations. The game-tested altcoin still has a massively over-minted supply of 5.5 trillion tokens in circulation, a figure normally ascribed to meme coins.

$LUNC AND $USTC BURN UPDATE pic.twitter.com/hboLcOFNdN

However, the LUNC chain restoration efforts have torched nearly 500 billion out of the original pile, showcasing what years of strict coordination can do for an embattled community.

Aside from the 19% portion claimed by Binance, the on-chain transaction tax and direct LUNC community burns make up the rest of the historical total. Regardless, the biggest hurdle for a proper Terra Luna Classic (LUNC) restoration point remains the stagnant trading volume.

Since the hard fork in May 2022, the cumulative burn efforts crunched 6.7% of total supply so far.

Token burning is a considerable deflation measure for long-term holders to ensure the asset remains at relatively bearable market value.

#5172. XRP, ETH, DOGE: Tim Draper Predicts Bitcoin to Absorb Altcoins

Billionaire and longtime Bitcoin (BTC) supporter Tim Draper predicts a future where Bitcoin absorbs the innovations of other altcoins.

Draper's statement has major implications for big cryptocurrencies like XRP, Ethereum (ETH) and Dogecoin (DOGE), which have built their own ecosystems and now compete in the wider cryptocurrency market.

In a recent post on X, Draper compared Bitcoin's rising dominance to Microsoft's path to becoming the dominant software company, as the company absorbed successful innovations from other businesses.

"Microsoft never invented the spreadsheet, word processor, or slide deck. They absorbed them," stated Draper.

The billionaire investor brought up pioneering software like Lotus 1-2-3 and WordPerfect, which eventually saw their dominance give way to Microsoft's Excel and Word.

Draper predicts that this could apply to the cryptocurrency industry, as Bitcoin could become the ultimate absorber of innovations from alternative blockchain networks.

"Altcoins are the Lotus 1-2-3s and WordPerfects of this time," wrote Draper.

Smaller cryptocurrencies are thus essentially incubators for technological innovations that could eventually become part of the bigger Bitcoin ecosystem, according to Draper.

"They show that it works, then the innovation that pulls the strongest gets ported to the Bitcoin platform," he added.

Draper also supported his statement by citing the rising market share of Bitcoin in the overall cryptocurrency market.

The billionaire investor recalled observing how Bitcoin dominance rose from about 40% to 50% and then even further to 61%.

"Bitcoin is that platform," said Draper, claiming that the biggest cryptocurrency could follow a path similar to Microsoft's.

This statement has implications for the future of big altcoins like Ethereum, XRP and Dogecoin.

Ethereum is the leading smart contract and dApp platform, while XRP is mainly known for its cross-border payments and the wider XRP Ledger ecosystem. Meanwhile, Dogecoin is one of the most iconic meme coins in the cryptocurrency market.

Still, the growing ecosystem around Bitcoin, which adds more scaling and programmability through solutions such as the Lightning Network and projects like Stacks, shows how Bitcoin is trying to expand beyond its basic use case as a cryptocurrency.

#5171. Bitcoin’s spot-demand signal is improving. A bigger futures slump is drowning it out

Both demand components remain negative, leaving Bitcoin’s tentative stabilization dependent on persistent spot improvement and easing futures weakness.

Bitcoin’s improving spot-demand signal is losing ground to a sharper deterioration in perpetual futures. With the price near $73,000, that split leaves its attempted recovery vulnerable ahead of Friday’s US producer-inflation release.

Bitcoin traded around $73,478 at about 10:40 UTC on November 12. A Coinbase quote one minute earlier was about $73,469.

In a November 11 CryptoQuant note, analyst RugaResearch put the combined 30-day spot-and-perpetual demand estimate at negative 348,000 BTC on November 10, its weakest reading that month.

From November 7 to November 10, the spot proxy improved by approximately 29,500 BTC. The perpetual-futures estimate deteriorated by about 63,500 BTC over the same period. Both components remained below zero, leaving the improvement short of positive readings.

RugaResearch’s November 10 snapshot shows opposing three-day changes inside a still-negative 30-day demand estimate.

Perpetual deterioration was more than twice the spot improvement; both component levels remained negative.

Analyst proxy, not executed purchases or capital flows. Both component levels remained negative. Exact formula, venue coverage and absolute component levels are not disclosed. Each rolling reading adds a day and drops the oldest.

Reported approximate changes from November 7 to 10. Desktop arrow lengths use 1 pixel per 200 BTC; mobile uses 1 pixel per 500 BTC. Signed changes share a zero-change reference within each view. The combined level is a separate reported figure; no net-change calculation or component baseline is inferred.

Analyst proxy, not executed purchases or capital flows. Both component levels remained negative. Exact formula, venue coverage and absolute component levels are not disclosed. Each rolling reading adds a day and drops the oldest.

This is an analyst’s proxy, not a purchase count. Its public description omits the formula, exchange coverage and absolute component levels.

Thanks for signing up to The Catalyst. Look out for your first daily email. If you already receive it, you’re all set.

Each reading adds a day and drops the oldest. That rolling window makes persistence across several closes more informative than one improvement.

This breakdown also differs from the apparent-demand measure discussed in CryptoSlate’s November 11 coverage. That article’s negative 284,000-BTC reading cannot be compared with negative 348,000 BTC as though one followed the other in a single series.

RugaResearch said spot moving toward zero, easing futures weakness and a flattening total while price holds its lows would strengthen the range case.

A separate CryptoQuant note by CoinNiel reported approximately 9,956 BTC in net exchange inflows on November 10, followed by about 981 BTC in preliminary net outflows on November 11.

CoinNiel labeled the November 11 data preliminary. Exchange withdrawals do not necessarily represent purchases, nor deposits sales, so several days of consistent flows would offer a stronger signal than one reversal.

The same note reported an all-exchange funding rate of 0.0051% in preliminary November 11 data, down from 0.0113%. Longs were still paying shorts, but at a lower reported rate.

In CoinNiel’s November 11 snapshot, open interest rose 1.2% from the previous day to about $26 billion, close to its seven-day average through November 10. That combination indicates lower long funding costs alongside slightly higher outstanding exposure. Open interest includes long and short positions, so its increase does not identify which side is driving the demand split.

US spot Bitcoin ETFs supplied a separate caution. Farside’s latest completed row showed net outflows of $300 million on November 11. Smaller withdrawals or sustained inflows would strengthen the stabilization case, but the latest reading still points to withdrawals.

The Bureau of Labor Statistics schedules October’s Producer Price Index (PPI) for Friday, November 13, at 8:30 a.m. Eastern, or 13:30 UTC.

Inflation matters because it can change expectations for financing conditions. In his November 10 speech, delivered before that day’s consumer-inflation release, Fed Governor Christopher Waller said inflation remained too high and leaned toward a quarter-point December rate increase under his expected inflation path. That was his conditional view, rather than a commitment by the Federal Open Market Committee.

A PPI report that reinforces expectations of tighter policy could keep financing conditions restrictive and weigh on risk appetite. A softer report could ease that pressure.

Friday’s initial price reaction will leave the persistence test open. Renewed spot weakness or an accelerating futures drag would undermine stabilization, even if Bitcoin briefly rebounds after the release. Smaller ETF outflows and sustained exchange withdrawals would add context without replacing the component readings.
